## MAP1
```json
{
 "id": "monument",
 "name": "Monument",
 "act": "ACT I · INDUCTION",
 "intro": "Dummy Corporation welcomes you off the factory floor. The cars are your colleagues; Certification is your goal.",
 "tiles": [
  {
   "id": "A1",
   "n": 1,
   "title": "The welcome wall",
   "task": {
    "kind": "any"
   },
   "gift": null,
   "tale": "Fresh off the factory floor, you're ready to do some damage to yourself. Make your engineers proud."
  },
  {
   "id": "B1",
   "n": 2,
   "title": "The podium bridge",
   "task": {
    "kind": "dash"
   },
   "gift": {
    "node": "battery1"
   },
   "tale": "Battery 1 is fitted. The skybridge was built for shoppers who never came; staff watch you dash into a colleague."
  },
  {
   "id": "C1",
   "n": 3,
   "title": "The LED drum",
   "task": {
    "kind": "drop"
   },
   "gift": null,
   "tale": "The LED drum lights each bend. Step off the high road onto a car: gravity is company policy."
  },
  {
   "id": "C2",
   "n": 4,
   "title": "The portal tower",
   "task": {
    "kind": "level"
   },
   "gift": null,
   "tale": "The road runs through the portal tower at the sixth floor. Take your write-off up there; ignore the view."
  },
  {
   "id": "B2",
   "n": 5,
   "title": "The monument",
   "task": {
    "kind": "struck"
   },
   "gift": null,
   "tale": "The plaque reads ZERO ACCIDENTS: a target, not a record. Stand near it until a colleague finds you."
  },
  {
   "id": "A2",
   "n": 6,
   "title": "The neon shops",
   "task": {
    "kind": "cone"
   },
   "gift": {
    "node": "hands1"
   },
   "tale": "The neon shops sell nothing, but the traffic reads them. Hands 1 is fitted: bait a colleague with a cone."
  },
  {
   "id": "A3",
   "n": 7,
   "title": "The stacked cubes",
   "task": {
    "kind": "combo",
    "param": "story.task.comboEasy"
   },
   "gift": null,
   "tale": "The stacked cubes rose one budget at a time. Build the same way: one hit, then another, quickly."
  },
  {
   "id": "B3",
   "n": 8,
   "title": "The LED canyon",
   "task": {
    "kind": "jump"
   },
   "gift": {
    "node": "legs1"
   },
   "tale": "Two screens advertise to each other all night. Legs 1 is fitted: jump into a car between them."
  },
  {
   "id": "C3",
   "n": 9,
   "title": "The LED gateway",
   "task": {
    "kind": "bounce"
   },
   "gift": null,
   "tale": "The gateway thanked the car that wrote you off for driving safely. Let one colleague throw you into the next."
  }
 ],
 "taskLabels": {
  "any": "ANY WRITE-OFF",
  "dash": "DASH INTO A CAR",
  "drop": "DROP ONTO A CAR",
  "jump": "JUMP INTO A CAR",
  "level": "ON A RAISED ROAD",
  "cone": "CONE-BAITED CAR",
  "push": "PUSH OFF A CAR",
  "bounce": "THROWN INTO A CAR",
  "struck": "STRUCK, NOT DASHING",
  "combo": "COMBO ×{n}",
  "cls": "A {cls}"
 }
}
```

  "label": "Rear share",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.05,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rear impact zone.",
  "purpose": "Counts as contact for Non-compliant, pays almost nothing",
  "source": "GDD §8.3"
 },
 {
  "id": "rewards.writeOff",
  "label": "Write-off bonus",
  "unit": "points",
  "default": 2500,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Score reward only, never chassis damage.",
  "purpose": "Larger than any single impact, so finishing a body always beats farming one",
  "source": "GDD §8.3"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.comboWindow",
  "label": "Combo window",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0.2,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "A hit within this many seconds of the last hit chains a combo.",
  "purpose": "Two dashes, or a bounce into the next car",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.comboStep",
  "label": "Combo step",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Each chained hit scores 1 + this × (links − 1) times its points.",
  "purpose": "Second hit ×1.5, third ×2",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.comboMax",
  "label": "Combo cap",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 5,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "The combo multiplier's ceiling. P6.5–6.8: 3. P6.9: 5, with scoring.comboVariety.",
  "purpose": "×3 at the fifth hit",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.widerView",
  "label": "Wider view width",
  "unit": "degrees",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Effective cone width when owned.",
  "purpose": "Roughly human binocular plus near peripheral; the first sense returned",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.hipsTurnRate",
  "label": "Hips turn rate",
  "unit": "degrees/s",
  "default": 360,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Effective turn rate when owned. Under DIRECT (P6.5) Hips has no job and leaves the shop (D48).",
  "purpose": "Halves the cost of looking without removing it",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2; Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.spineLimit",
  "label": "Spine steering limit",
  "unit": "degrees",
  "default": 45,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Maximum lunge bend.",
  "purpose": "Enough to correct a misread, not enough to home in",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2; Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.spineRate",
  "label": "Spine steering rate",
  "unit": "degrees/s",
  "default": 540,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Maximum rate within the bend limit.",
  "purpose": "The full 45° bend fits inside the lunge duration",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2; Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.neckSpeed",
  "label": "Neck decoupled-cone speed fraction",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.8,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Movement rate while the view cone is held off the facing; Neck no longer governs sidestep, which is Strafe.",
  "purpose": "Moving while looking elsewhere costs speed",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpWindow",
  "label": "Jump input window",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Time from lunge start for second A press.",
  "purpose": "Early in the lunge only; jumping is a decision, not a correction",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpDistance",
  "label": "Jump extension",
  "unit": "px",
  "default": 112,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Jump (Legs, B): ground covered in the air, along the input (else the facing). Leg damage shortens it as it shortens the dash.",
  "purpose": "Clears a car lengthwise and a planter world.jumpableDepth deep",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2; Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 76 (27 October): Prototype 6.6 and the jump test"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpAirtime",
  "label": "Jump airtime",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Jump airtime in P6.5–6.7 and the jump test's reference. From P6.8 the Legs level sets it: abilities.legs.airtimeBase + abilities.legs.airtimeStep × (level − 1). Ground covered = take-off speed × airtime.",
  "purpose": "A running jump clears a sedan broadside; a dash jump clears a van broadside",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2; Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 76 (27 October): Prototype 6.6 and the jump test; Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpHeight",
  "label": "Jump height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 44,
  "min": 10,
  "max": 120,
  "about": "Top of the jump arc (a parabola over upgrades.jumpAirtime), the same at any speed. The dummy clears a car only while above its roof, and a jumpable piece above world.jumpableHeight.",
  "purpose": "Over a sedan (19.8) and a van (30.6) at vehicles.scale 0.9; not a bus (43.2)",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 76 (27 October): Prototype 6.6 and the jump test"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpMaxSpeed",
  "label": "Jump top speed",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 324,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Cap on the jump's take-off speed. A jump during a dash (B in a dash) takes off at the dash's speed, capped here: the long jump.",
  "purpose": "Dash jump covers 162: clears a van broadside, not a bus",
  "source": "Edit 76 (27 October): Prototype 6.6 and the jump test"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpAirAccel",
  "label": "Jump air steering",
  "unit": "world units/s²",
  "default": 324,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A normal jump turns its velocity toward the input by at most this per second, at its take-off speed (at least walking pace). No input: momentum holds. A dash jump does not steer.",
  "purpose": "Corrects a jump by about a third of its speed; cannot reverse it",
  "source": "Edit 76 (27 October): Prototype 6.6 and the jump test"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.jump.runToClear",
  "label": "Run to clear",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: only a running jump or a dash jump clears anything. A standing or walking jump hops: the body rises, but meets cars, barriers and rails as on the ground. 0: any jump clears what its arc clears (P6.6).",
  "purpose": "Speed is the skill: walk and jump, but run to clear",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.abilities",
  "label": "Ability tracks",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "0: the P6.5–6.6 tree. 1: four tracks by level (P6.7–6.8: Battery, Legs, Arms, Throw). 2: three tracks of three levels (P6.9: Battery, Legs, Arms; the throw is Arms 2, the hitch Arms 3). 3: four tracks (P6.10: Battery, Legs, Hands of two levels, Arms the push).",
  "purpose": "One track per button; levels shield the ability from damage",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9; Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.maxLevel",
  "label": "Ability levels",
  "unit": "levels",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 4,
  "about": "Levels per ability track. Level 1 grants the ability (Battery: a faster charge). P6.9: three tracks (Battery, Legs, Arms), three levels.",
  "purpose": "\"We only have 3 upgrade levels per asset\" (user, 27 October)",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities; Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.wearCut",
  "label": "Wear shield per level",
  "unit": "share per level",
  "default": 0.25,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 0.34,
  "about": "Each level of a track cuts its body part's wear on that ability by this share. Battery shields the recharge from torso damage; Legs the run, dash and jump from leg damage; Arms the grip, Throw the throw, from arm damage.",
  "purpose": "Level 3 cuts wear by 75%",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities; Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.battery.rechargeStep",
  "label": "Battery recharge step",
  "unit": "ratio per level",
  "default": 0.8,
  "min": 0.5,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Each Battery level multiplies battery.secondsPerCharge by this: 0.8 s, then 0.64, 0.51, 0.41 at level 3.",
  "purpose": "Faster dash recharge, a dash about every 0.4 s at level 3",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities; Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "economy.price.ability.level1",
  "label": "Ability level 1 price",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Write-offs (credits) for level 1 of any track. Throw needs Arms level 1.",
  "purpose": "Every button's verb for one write-off",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "economy.price.ability.level2",
  "label": "Ability level 2 price",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Write-offs for level 2 of any track; needs level 1.",
  "purpose": "A run of 9 write-offs buys four level 1s and two level 2s",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "economy.price.ability.level3",
  "label": "Ability level 3 price",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Write-offs for level 3 of any track; needs level 2.",
  "purpose": "Maxing every track takes 24: several runs",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.torsoRechargeLoss",
  "label": "Torso recharge loss",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Torso damage slows the battery's recharge: rate × (1 − this × torso share × (1 − abilities.wearCut × Battery level)).",
  "purpose": "Gives torso damage a cost under DIRECT (D46)",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.torsoRechargeFloor",
  "label": "Torso recharge floor",
  "unit": "share",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "The recharge never falls below this share of its rate.",
  "purpose": "A worn torso still recharges",
  "source": "Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.battery.rechargeCut",
  "label": "Battery recharge step",
  "unit": "s per level",
  "default": 0.1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 0.19,
  "about": "Each Battery level takes this off battery.secondsPerCharge: 0.8 s, then 0.7, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4 at level 4.",
  "purpose": "Linear, like the other tracks; level 4 still empties in a long dash chain",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.legs.airtimeBase",
  "label": "Legs 1 airtime",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Time in the air at Legs 1. The height stays upgrades.jumpHeight.",
  "purpose": "A running jump clears a handrail; a dash jump a sedan",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.legs.airtimeStep",
  "label": "Legs airtime step",
  "unit": "s per level",
  "default": 0.1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 0.3,
  "about": "Each Legs level after the first adds this to the airtime: 0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6 s.",
  "purpose": "Legs 2 a dash jump clears a van; Legs 3 a running jump a sedan",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.conesPerLevel",
  "label": "Cones per Arms level",
  "unit": "cones per level",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Cones the dummy carries per Arms level: 1, 2, 3, 4. X grabs while there is room; else it places the top cone.",
  "purpose": "A stack to lay a line of traps",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.throw.distanceStep",
  "label": "Throw range step",
  "unit": "world units per level",
  "default": 40,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 120,
  "about": "Each Throw level after the first adds this to upgrades.throwDistance: 120, 160, 200, 240.",
  "purpose": "Linear range, as asked",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "economy.price.ability.level4",
  "label": "Ability level 4 price",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 4,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Write-offs for level 4 of any track; needs level 3. A track costs 10 in all.",
  "purpose": "Maxing one track takes about a run",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8; Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.bounce.newton",
  "label": "Newtonian bounce",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the bounce off a car is the dummy's full post-impact velocity (momentum, proto.restitution, proto.contactFriction; proto.recoilScale 1), then a slide slowed by physics.slideFriction; control returns under walking pace after proto.recoilSeconds. 0: the P2–6.7 recoil (scaled, fading over proto.recoilSeconds).",
  "purpose": "Ricochet as physics, not an upgrade",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "physics.slideFriction",
  "label": "Slide friction",
  "unit": "coefficient",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "A knocked dummy slides to a stop at this × physics.gravity × proto.scale.unitsPerMetre (about 120 u/s² at 1).",
  "purpose": "A head-on throw slides about 127 units; a flank bounce about 8",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "physics.gravity",
  "label": "Gravity",
  "unit": "m/s²",
  "default": 9.81,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 20,
  "about": "Standard gravity, for the slide.",
  "purpose": "Physical constant",
  "source": "Edit 78 (27 October): Prototype 6.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.dummyMass",
  "label": "Dummy mass",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.056,
  "min": 0.01,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "The dummy's mass relative to a sedan (vehicles.sedan.mass 1): about 56 kg to a tonne. Sets the momentum exchange in a hit.",
  "purpose": "A light body bounces off a heavy car",
  "source": "Registered Edit 78 from the sim's code (P3 onward)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.restitution",
  "label": "Restitution",
  "unit": "coefficient",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Coefficient of restitution of a dummy–car hit: 0 sticks, 1 bounces back at the closing speed.",
  "purpose": "A dull, plastic hit",
  "source": "Registered Edit 78 from the sim's code (P3 onward)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.contactFriction",
  "label": "Contact friction",
  "unit": "coefficient",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Friction along the contact in a hit: how much a moving car's side drags the dummy along.",
  "purpose": "A flank drags the dummy with the car",
  "source": "Registered Edit 78 from the sim's code (P3 onward)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.recoilScale",
  "label": "Recoil scale",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.7,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Share of the physical post-impact velocity the dummy keeps. P2–6.7: 0.6. P6.8 (proto.bounce.newton): 1.",
  "purpose": "Full momentum from P6.8",
  "source": "Registered Edit 78 from the sim's code (P3 onward); Edit 83 (28 October): Prototype 7.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.recoilSeconds",
  "label": "Recoil time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.35,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "P2–6.7: the recoil fades over this. P6.8: the least time out of control after a hit.",
  "purpose": "A beat before the player steers again",
  "source": "Registered Edit 78 from the sim's code (P3 onward)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.dummyStrikeSpeed",
  "label": "Strike speed cap",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 100,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 400,
  "about": "The dummy's own speed counted into a hit is capped here (a dash moves far faster).",
  "purpose": "Keeps a dash's damage and bounce in range",
  "source": "Registered Edit 78 from the sim's code (P3 onward)"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.battery.level1Seconds",
  "label": "Battery 1 charge time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.8,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Seconds a dash charge takes at Battery 1.",
  "purpose": "\"battery makes it 0.8, 0.7, 0.6 seconds\"",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.battery.level2Seconds",
  "label": "Battery 2 charge time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.7,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Seconds a dash charge takes at Battery 2.",
  "purpose": "As asked",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.battery.level3Seconds",
  "label": "Battery 3 charge time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.6,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Seconds a dash charge takes at Battery 3.",
  "purpose": "As asked",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "input.throwHoldSeconds",
  "label": "Throw hold",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Holding X this long with a cone throws it (Arms 2). A shorter press puts it down.",
  "purpose": "\"Level 2 - long press to throw\"",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.hitchReach",
  "label": "Hitch reach",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 12,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 60,
  "about": "Arms 3: X grabs a moving car whose side is within this of the body.",
  "purpose": "Forgiving at speed",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.hitchSeconds",
  "label": "Hitch time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0.3,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "The longest ride before the grip gives.",
  "purpose": "Long enough to pick a target",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.hitchMinSpeed",
  "label": "Hitch speed",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 30,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 200,
  "about": "A car slower than this cannot be hitched.",
  "purpose": "Stopped cars are targets, not rides",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.hitchEdge",
  "label": "Hitch edge",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 40,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 200,
  "about": "The grip lets go this close to the screen's edge.",
  "purpose": "The sling lands on this screen",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.slingScale",
  "label": "Sling speed",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Letting go throws the dummy at the car's velocity × this. The flight is a knock: it slides and strikes as a dash.",
  "purpose": "Newtonian: you leave at the car's speed",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.slingDash",
  "label": "Sling dash",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 400,
  "about": "A while hitched lets go with a dash: this much speed along the stick, for one charge.",
  "purpose": "Aim the sling",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.comboVariety",
  "label": "Combo variety",
  "unit": "per kind",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Each new kind of move in a chain adds this to the multiplier: dash, jump, bounce, sling, drop, cone, struck.",
  "purpose": "Mix moves to climb faster",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.comboAirPause",
  "label": "Combo air time",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the combo clock stops while the dummy jumps, flies from a hit, rides a hitch or drops. 0: it always runs.",
  "purpose": "Air and physics keep a chain alive",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.coneLink",
  "label": "Cone link",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a thrown cone that hits a moving car links the combo, once a car.",
  "purpose": "Throws join the chain",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.coneHitPoints",
  "label": "Cone hit points",
  "unit": "points",
  "default": 20,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 200,
  "about": "Points for a cone link, × the multiplier.",
  "purpose": "Small: a link, not a payday",
  "source": "Edit 79 (27 October): Prototype 6.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.stealthUnseen",
  "label": "Stealth grade: unseen",
  "unit": "multiplier",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "Test road: a damaging strike the striking car never noticed this pass (no true sighting: its own sensors or a street camera, noticed or in its path) scores its damage times this. 0: no stealth grade (10.1–10.7). 10.8 sets 3",
  "purpose": "Test road: a damaging strike the striking car never noticed this pass (no true sighting: its own sensors or a street camera, noticed or in its path) scores its damage times this. 0: no stealth grade (10.1–10.7). 10.8 sets 3",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS rows 725, 728"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.stealthLost",
  "label": "Stealth grade: lost",
  "unit": "multiplier",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "Test road: a damaging strike the car had noticed this pass but lost before the dummy sprang scores its damage times this. 10.8 sets 2",
  "purpose": "Test road: a damaging strike the car had noticed this pass but lost before the dummy sprang scores its damage times this. 10.8 sets 2",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS rows 725, 728"
 },
 {
  "id": "scoring.stealthSeen",
  "label": "Stealth grade: seen",
  "unit": "multiplier",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "Test road: a damaging strike the car had in sight as the dummy sprang scores its damage times this. 10.8 sets 1",
  "purpose": "Test road: a damaging strike the car had in sight as the dummy sprang scores its damage times this. 10.8 sets 1",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS rows 725, 728"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.hands.maxLevel",
  "label": "Hands levels",
  "unit": "levels",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Hands has two levels. There is no Hands 3: two hands.",
  "purpose": "\"There is no Level three (you only have two hands)\"",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.hands.conesPerLevel",
  "label": "Cones per Hands level",
  "unit": "cones per level",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Each Hands level carries this many more cones: Hands 1 one, Hands 2 two.",
  "purpose": "\"L1, pick up and drop one, L2, pick up and drop two\"",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "input.dropHoldSeconds",
  "label": "Put-down hold",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Holding X this long with a cone puts it down. A tap picks up.",
  "purpose": "\"tap once to pick up, press for 0.3s to drop\"",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.pushSpeed",
  "label": "Push speed",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 600,
  "about": "Arms 1: Y adds this speed, away from the car just hit or touched (or along the stick).",
  "purpose": "A ricochet you time",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.pushStep",
  "label": "Push step",
  "unit": "world units/s per level",
  "default": 40,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 200,
  "about": "Each Arms level after the first adds this to the push: 160, 200, 240.",
  "purpose": "\"Stronger arms let your ricochet be stronger\"",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.pushesPerLevel",
  "label": "Pushes per Arms level",
  "unit": "pushes per level",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Pushes before landing, per Arms level: 1, 2, 3. They refill on the ground.",
  "purpose": "\"one extra push each time so you can time it to ricochet more\"",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.pushReach",
  "label": "Push reach",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 10,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 40,
  "about": "Y pushes off a car whose side is within this of the body.",
  "purpose": "Touching counts, not only hitting",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "abilities.arms.pushWindow",
  "label": "Push window",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Y within this of a contact pushes off that car, wherever the body has bounced to.",
  "purpose": "Time the push after the hit",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.armPushLoss",
  "label": "armPushLoss",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Push speed lost per arm damage share.",
  "purpose": "Arm damage weakens the push",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.armPushFloor",
  "label": "armPushFloor",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "The push never falls below this share.",
  "purpose": "A broken arm still pushes",
  "source": "Edit 80 (27 October): Prototype 6.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.ricochetScale",
  "label": "Ricochet bounce",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "Ricochet (Legs, needs Jump): the bounce off a hit goes this many times as fast, so this many times as far.",
  "purpose": "Carry the bounce into the next car for a combo",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.jumpRecovery",
  "label": "Jump landing recovery",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Jump: after landing, no dash and no jump for this long. The landing keeps the jump's speed; movement does not stop.",
  "purpose": "Added to lunge recovery, so jumping is the most exposed action",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2; Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5; Edit 76 (27 October): Prototype 6.6 and the jump test"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.carrySpeed",
  "label": "Carry speed fraction",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.85,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Ground-travel modifier while carrying a cone.",
  "purpose": "Carrying a cone is a visible, minor handicap",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.holdSeconds",
  "label": "Nominal ride limit",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Safe-detachment exception remains a design/testing issue.",
  "purpose": "About one block of travel; a repositioning tool, not a free ride",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.throwDistance",
  "label": "Throw distance",
  "unit": "px",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Prop travel; wear scaling remains open.",
  "purpose": "Places a cone across one lane from the hard shoulder",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.bigCapacity",
  "label": "Big battery capacity",
  "unit": "charge units",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Effective capacity when Big battery is owned. Base is battery.capacity (2).",
  "purpose": "One dash more than base: a feint and a strike (D26)",
  "source": "GDD §5.2, 5.6, 9.2; Edit 40 (D26); Edit 77 (27 October): Prototype 6.7, abilities"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.kineticThreshold",
  "label": "Kinetic charge threshold",
  "unit": "damage units",
  "default": 300,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Candidate uses pre-bonus impact damage.",
  "purpose": "Only a committed hit refills; glancing contact does not",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.kineticFraction",
  "label": "Kinetic refill fraction",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Fraction of effective capacity.",
  "purpose": "Half a battery back, so a good hit buys the next attempt",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "upgrades.fastChargeMultiplier",
  "label": "Fast charge rate multiplier",
  "unit": "multiplier",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Multiplies rate, not seconds per unit.",
  "purpose": "Halves the wait, which is the only thing waiting ever costs",
  "source": "GDD §§5.2, 5.6, 5.8, 9.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.reactionDelay",
  "label": "Reaction delay",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Vehicle sensing response delay.",
  "purpose": "The window a bait must fit inside; 0.3 s is fair at a glance (P2.2)",
  "source": "GDD §6.2–6.3; Edit 37 (P2.2)"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideRadius",
  "label": "Side-sensor reach",
  "unit": "px",
  "default": 100,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Short side sensors at the nose.",
  "purpose": "Short enough that passing close is safe; long enough to make a car hesitate",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideNoticeSeconds",
  "label": "Side notice time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.6,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "Rung 2's side sensors notice a dummy in the driver's field (proto.driver.cautionZone either side of its path) after it has stayed there this long. Weaker than the front (perception.reactionDelay): strong front, weak edges (handover v2 §13, §17)",
  "purpose": "Rung 2's side sensors notice a dummy in the driver's field (proto.driver.cautionZone either side of its path) after it has stayed there this long. Weaker than the front (perception.reactionDelay): strong front, weak edges (handover v2 §13, §17)",
  "source": "GDD Edit 103 (Prototype 10.3): handover v2 §17; DECISIONS rows 673–678"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideCloseRange",
  "label": "Side field: close range",
  "unit": "px",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 720,
  "about": "Rung 2 up: a dummy in the field within this distance of the car's nose (beside it, its gap from the flank) is CLOSE: about the dash's reach. The car slows to perception.sideCloseSpeed; the notice meter fills at full speed (perception.sideNoticeSeconds). 0: no stages, the whole field alike (10.3–10.7). 10.8 sets 180",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up: a dummy in the field within this distance of the car's nose (beside it, its gap from the flank) is CLOSE: about the dash's reach. The car slows to perception.sideCloseSpeed; the notice meter fills at full speed (perception.sideNoticeSeconds). 0: no stages, the whole field alike (10.3–10.7). 10.8 sets 180",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideWaryRange",
  "label": "Side field: wary range",
  "unit": "px",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 720,
  "about": "Rung 2 up: a dummy in the field beyond perception.sideCloseRange but within this distance of the car's nose is WARY: the car slows to perception.sideWarySpeed; its notice meter fills at perception.sideWaryNoticeRate. Beyond, to the field's end, AWARE. 10.8 sets 360",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up: a dummy in the field beyond perception.sideCloseRange but within this distance of the car's nose is WARY: the car slows to perception.sideWarySpeed; its notice meter fills at perception.sideWaryNoticeRate. Beyond, to the field's end, AWARE. 10.8 sets 360",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideAwareSpeed",
  "label": "Side field: aware speed",
  "unit": "fraction",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rung 2 up, staged: AWARE (in the field, beyond perception.sideWaryRange), the car's speed is capped at this share of its cruise. 10.8 sets 0.85",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up, staged: AWARE (in the field, beyond perception.sideWaryRange), the car's speed is capped at this share of its cruise. 10.8 sets 0.85",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideWarySpeed",
  "label": "Side field: wary speed",
  "unit": "fraction",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rung 2 up, staged: WARY, the car's speed is capped at this share of its cruise. 10.8 sets 0.6",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up, staged: WARY, the car's speed is capped at this share of its cruise. 10.8 sets 0.6",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideCloseSpeed",
  "label": "Side field: close speed",
  "unit": "fraction",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rung 2 up, staged: CLOSE, the car's speed is capped at this share of its cruise, until it notices the dummy and stops. 10.8 sets 0.35",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up, staged: CLOSE, the car's speed is capped at this share of its cruise, until it notices the dummy and stops. 10.8 sets 0.35",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideAwareNoticeRate",
  "label": "Side field: aware notice rate",
  "unit": "fraction",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rung 2 up, staged: AWARE, the notice meter fills at this share of its speed: noticed after perception.sideNoticeSeconds divided by it. 0: aware, but it never notices so far out. 10.8 sets 0.5 (1.2 s; 0 cut the crawl's lead and let the cover springs back in)",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up, staged: AWARE, the notice meter fills at this share of its speed: noticed after perception.sideNoticeSeconds divided by it. 0: aware, but it never notices so far out. 10.8 sets 0.5 (1.2 s; 0 cut the crawl's lead and let the cover springs back in)",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "perception.sideWaryNoticeRate",
  "label": "Side field: wary notice rate",
  "unit": "fraction",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rung 2 up, staged: WARY, the notice meter fills at this share of its speed: noticed after perception.sideNoticeSeconds divided by it. 10.8 sets 1 (a slower WARY notice let the cover springs back in)",
  "purpose": "Rung 2 up, staged: WARY, the notice meter fills at this share of its speed: noticed after perception.sideNoticeSeconds divided by it. 10.8 sets 1 (a slower WARY notice let the cover springs back in)",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 730"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.timeGap",
  "label": "Following time gap",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal non-bus time gap.",
  "purpose": "Standard following distance; sets how often a usable gap appears",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.busTimeGap",
  "label": "Bus following time gap",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.8,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Bus following time gap.",
  "purpose": "Buses leave larger gaps, making them the easiest class to reach",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.minimumGap",
  "label": "Stopped gap",
  "unit": "px",
  "default": 8,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Does not alone prove safe admission.",
  "purpose": "Queued cars stay separable, so the dummy can enter a stopped line",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.acceleration",
  "label": "Acceleration",
  "unit": "px/s²",
  "default": 360,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Pulling away.",
  "purpose": "A stopped car takes about half a second to become dangerous again",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.comfortBraking",
  "label": "Comfortable braking",
  "unit": "px/s²",
  "default": 400,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal slowing; terrain affects safe stopping.",
  "purpose": "Normal slowing; the deceleration an incident is measured against",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.hardBraking",
  "label": "Hard braking",
  "unit": "px/s²",
  "default": 600,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Maximum nominal emergency deceleration.",
  "purpose": "Emergency stop; the cue that the player forced a reaction",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.arrivalTie",
  "label": "Arrival tie window",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Near-simultaneous arrival window.",
  "purpose": "Arrivals this close count as simultaneous at a junction",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.stopPause",
  "label": "Stop-sign pause",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Full-stop dwell.",
  "purpose": "A full stop long enough to be a reliable target",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.deadlockTimeout",
  "label": "Wait-cycle timeout",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Triggers safe recovery planning, not automatic reversing.",
  "purpose": "How long a wait cycle runs before safe recovery is planned",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation, §6.3 detection"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.sports.cruiseSpeed",
  "label": "Sports cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",
  "default": 192,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed; changing this is not changing the fixed damage-reference speed.",
  "purpose": "Fastest class; highest damage, smallest window",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation tuning"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.hatchback.cruiseSpeed",
  "label": "Hatchback cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",
  "default": 156,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed; changing this is not changing the fixed damage-reference speed.",
  "purpose": "Fast and recommits once, so timing is readable but tight",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation tuning"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.wagon.cruiseSpeed",
  "label": "Wagon cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",
  "default": 144,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed; changing this is not changing the fixed damage-reference speed.",
  "purpose": "Wide clearance at moderate speed; rewards a fresh body's full reach",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation tuning"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.sedan.cruiseSpeed",
  "label": "Sedan cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",
  "default": 138,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed; changing this is not changing the fixed damage-reference speed.",
  "purpose": "The reference vehicle; all damage figures normalise to a sedan at cruise",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation tuning"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.van.cruiseSpeed",
  "label": "Van cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed; changing this is not changing the fixed damage-reference speed.",
  "purpose": "Slow but heavy; brakes then swerves late",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation tuning"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.bus.cruiseSpeed",
  "label": "Bus cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",
  "default": 108,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed; changing this is not changing the fixed damage-reference speed.",
  "purpose": "Slowest and heaviest; cannot swerve, so it is the beginner's target",
  "source": "GDD §6.2 navigation tuning"
 },
 {

  "default": 0.5,

 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

  "source": "GDD §7.1"
 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

  "purpose": "One above the quota sum of 14, so minimum performance never certifies",
  "source": "GDD §§10.3, 10.6"
 },
 {
  "id": "run.appearanceCeiling",
  "label": "Appearance ceiling",
  "unit": "vehicles",
  "default": 1000,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Ceiling, never a session-length target.",
  "purpose": "A ceiling that bounds the run, never a pacing target",
  "source": "GDD §§10.3, 10.6"
 },
 {
  "id": "feedback.hitStop",
  "label": "Hit-stop duration",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.06,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Applies to the next impact pause; an active pause retains its duration.",
  "purpose": "Short enough not to break flow, long enough to register the hit",
  "source": "GDD §§8.3, 5.5"
 },
 {

  "unit": "s",

  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Approximate reported duration; presentation and run sequencing interact.",
  "purpose": "The destruction animation; gameplay is frozen throughout",
  "source": "GDD §§8.3, 5.5"
 },
 {

 },

 },

 {

  "about": "Long enough that cautious play is never punished; short enough that standing still is",

 },
 {

  "unit": "s",
  "default": 5,

 },
 {
  "id": "service.restoreFraction",
  "label": "restoreFraction",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Full restoration. A partial repair would be a gentler punishment and a worse joke",
  "purpose": "Full restoration. A partial repair would be a gentler punishment and a worse joke",
  "source": "GDD §8.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.laneWidth",
  "label": "Lane width",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 56,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Level-geometry input. Lunge reach is tuned against a two-lane road plus verge.",
  "purpose": "One traffic lane; two lanes plus verge is what lunge reach is tuned against",
  "source": "GDD §§7.3, 7.9"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.fallRecovery",
  "label": "Fall recovery",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Time cost of falling into a hole or water. No damage and no write-off.",
  "purpose": "What a fall costs: no damage, no write-off, only this",
  "source": "GDD §§7.5, 7.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.occludedFractionMax",
  "label": "Occlusion ceiling",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of the play area that may block the player's read of the fleet. Static terrain and props only; vehicles are transient.",

  "source": "GDD §7.9 clause 6; Edit 39 (city at 25%)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.barrierGapsMin",
  "label": "Minimum barrier gaps",
  "unit": "count",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Pedestrian openings per side of a barrier run.",
  "purpose": "Minimum pedestrian openings per side of a barrier run",
  "source": "GDD §7.9 clause 2"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.spawnLeadIn",
  "label": "Spawn lead-in",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 200,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Distance outside the play edge where an appearance is placed.",
  "purpose": "Distance outside the play edge where a car is placed, so it enters at cruise speed and correctly spaced",
  "source": "GDD §7.9 clause 3"
 },
 {
  "id": "damage.plateMultiplier",
  "label": "Impact plate multiplier",
  "unit": "multiplier",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Score-only surface modifier. Affects impact points, never health loss or write-off count.",
  "purpose": "What a hit on a calibrated plate scores; the roundabout's scoring geometry",
  "source": "GDD §§7.3, 8.4"
 },
 {
  "id": "damage.deckMultiplier",
  "label": "Highway multiplier",
  "unit": "×",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "Points from a hit by a deck (highway) car are multiplied by this. P5.1: 1. Highway hits pay more only because the cars run traffic.deckSpeedScale faster.",
  "purpose": "Kept for tuning; the faster cars already pay (speed scale)² for the same hit",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October); retired in Edit 50.1"
 },
 {
  "id": "damage.walkShare",
  "label": "Walk share",
  "unit": "share",
  "default": 0.25,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of the dummy's walking or running speed that adds to the closing speed of a hit. A dash (or a thrown dummy) adds its full speed, up to proto.dummyStrikeSpeed.",
  "purpose": "Walking into a car scores little; the dash is the scoring verb",
  "source": "Edit 50.1 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.pivotOffset",
  "label": "Turn pivot offset",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Rear-axle steering. The turn centre sits this fraction of the vehicle's length behind its midpoint.",
  "purpose": "How far behind the midpoint the turn centre sits, as a fraction of length",
  "source": "GDD §6.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.lookAheadBase",
  "label": "Look-ahead, fixed part",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 20,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Constant term of the forward check. Total look-ahead is this plus lookAheadSeconds of travel.",
  "purpose": "The fixed part of how far a car checks ahead",
  "source": "GDD §6.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.lookAheadSeconds",

  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.45,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Speed-scaled term of the forward check, so fast classes look further.",

  "source": "GDD §6.2"
 },
 {

  "label": "Deadlock back-off",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1.0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,

  "source": "GDD §6.2 rule 7"
 },
 {

 },
 {

 },

 },
 {

 },
 {

  "label": "Police cruise speed",
  "unit": "px/s",

  "min": 0,

  "about": "Nominal class cruise speed.",
  "purpose": "Quick and deliberate; tracks the approach before committing",

 },
 {
  "id": "ui.artScale",

  "unit": "units",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,

 },
 {
  "id": "ui.typeFieldLabel",
  "label": "Field label cap height",
  "unit": "units",
  "default": 12,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,

  "purpose": "Legible at laptop distance (about 0.6 m) without covering the field",
  "source": "Edit 39 (tags at 50%)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.city",
  "label": "City statics",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "City blocks and jersey barriers are solid statics (Fix A). 0 restores the P2.2 roads.",
  "purpose": "Anything that looks solid is solid",
  "source": "Edit 38 §7.4 (decision 96)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.extrusion",
  "label": "Height extrusion",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.3,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of an object's height drawn as a screen-up offset.",
  "purpose": "Three-quarter depth without changing colliders",
  "source": "Edit 38 §4.1.3"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.stopCue",
  "label": "Stop bracket",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Each moving car shows where it will stop.",
  "purpose": "Shows the stop line a bait must beat",
  "source": "Edit 37 (decision 91)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.driver.coveredReaction",
  "label": "Covered reaction",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.18,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Reaction time while the driver already covers the brake.",
  "purpose": "A cautious car reacts faster than a cruising one",
  "source": "Edit 37"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.torsoFrom",
  "label": "torsoFrom",
  "unit": "damage",
  "default": 100,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Assessed damage that reaches the torso or an arm",
  "purpose": "Bumper-height hits land on the legs",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.headFrom",
  "label": "headFrom",
  "unit": "damage",
  "default": 250,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Assessed damage that reaches the head",
  "purpose": "Only the hardest hits reach the head",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.crackedShare",
  "label": "crackedShare",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of body health lost by one part to crack it",
  "purpose": "Cracks show before a part breaks",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.brokenShare",
  "label": "brokenShare",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.4,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of body health lost by one part to break it",
  "purpose": "A broken part is a warning-outlined fact",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.effects",
  "label": "effects",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Damaged parts cut abilities; 0 = display only",
  "purpose": "Damage you can feel, not only see",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.legSpeedLoss",
  "label": "legSpeedLoss",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Ground speed lost per leg share",
  "purpose": "Leg damage slows walking, running and carrying",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.legSpeedFloor",
  "label": "legSpeedFloor",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Lowest ground-speed multiplier",
  "purpose": "A wrecked body still moves",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.legDashLoss",
  "label": "legDashLoss",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Dash reach lost per leg share, to movement.wearFloor",
  "purpose": "Leg damage shortens the dash (D19)",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7; Edit 39 (D19)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.torsoTurnLoss",
  "label": "torsoTurnLoss",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Turn rate lost per torso share",
  "purpose": "Torso damage stiffens the turn",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.torsoTurnFloor",
  "label": "torsoTurnFloor",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Lowest turn-rate multiplier",
  "purpose": "A wrecked body still turns",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.headConeLoss",
  "label": "headConeLoss",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "View-cone width lost per head share",
  "purpose": "Head damage narrows what you resolve",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.headConeFloor",

  "max": 1,
  "about": "Lowest view-cone multiplier",

 },
 {

 },
 {
  "id": "proto.parts.armThrowFloor",

  "purpose": "A damaged arm still throws",
  "source": "Edit 38 §5.7"
 },
 {

 },
 {

  "source": "Edit 34; Edit 39"
 },
 {

  "about": "Bark bubble timing (Edit 34; text in content/db/fleet.json).",

 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

  "about": "Share of a screen's tiles carrying a puddle patch, on road and walkable ground.",

  "source": "Edit 40 §7.3 (D18)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.puddleSpotMax",
  "label": "Puddle spot size",
  "unit": "tiles",

  "purpose": "Spots read as puddles, not lakes",
  "source": "Edit 40 §7.3"
 },
 {

  "unit": "screens",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Screens across one level (D24).",
  "purpose": "A level is a 3 × 3 district: nine screens to explore",
  "source": "Edit 40 §7.1 (D24)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.districtRows",
  "label": "District rows",
  "unit": "screens",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Screens down one level (D24).",
  "purpose": "Row 1 lies on the river",
  "source": "Edit 40 §7.1 (D24)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.gateWidth",
  "label": "Pedestrian gate width",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 48,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Opening in a screen edge where the dummy may walk to the next screen.",
  "purpose": "Wider than world.pedestrianGap; fits the alleys between flush blocks",
  "source": "Edit 40 §7.1 (D24)"
 },
 {

  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.4,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Slide between screens. Play pauses for its length.",
  "purpose": "Long enough to read the direction; short enough to keep momentum",
  "source": "Edit 40 §7.1 (D24)"
 },
 {

  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.25,

  "about": "Target share of a screen covered by city blocks (generator).",
  "purpose": "Buildings about a quarter of each screen",
  "source": "Edit 39 §7.9 clause 6 (decision 118)"
 },
 {

  "label": "Jersey barrier share",

  "default": 0.08,
  "min": 0.05,
  "max": 0.1,
  "about": "Target share of the road edge lined by jersey barriers (generator).",
  "purpose": "Barriers on 5–10% of the road edge",
  "source": "Edit 39 §7.9 clause 6 (decision 118)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.district",
  "label": "Districts",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Each road is a 3 × 3 district of screens, Zelda-style. 0 = one static screen.",
  "purpose": "Exploration without a scrolling camera",
  "source": "Edit 40 (D24)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.district.seed",
  "label": "District seed",
  "unit": "seed",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Rerolls the eight generated screens of each district. 0 = the fixed layout.",
  "purpose": "Fixed layouts are learnable; a new seed gives a new street view",
  "source": "Edit 40 (D24)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.health.fragile",
  "label": "Fragile health",
  "unit": "health units",
  "default": 600,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Body health on the easy setting (Fragile).",
  "purpose": "Easy: fewer hits per write-off",
  "source": "Edit 40.1 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.health.standard",
  "label": "Standard health",
  "unit": "health units",
  "default": 800,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Body health on the base setting (Standard). Equals damage.bodyHealth.",
  "purpose": "The base contract",
  "source": "Edit 40.1 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.health.reinforced",
  "label": "Reinforced health",
  "unit": "health units",
  "default": 1000,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Body health on the difficult setting (Reinforced).",
  "purpose": "Difficult: more hits per write-off",
  "source": "Edit 40.1 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckHeight",
  "label": "Flyover height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Height of the low highway (Z1), about 10 m. A Z1 deck and everything on it draw lifted by height × proto.art.extrusion (18 art px). Reference height for stair runs and falls.",
  "purpose": "Lifted far enough that the south row of columns reads under the girder",
  "source": "Edit 41; Edits 43–44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckWidth",
  "label": "Flyover width",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Two lanes (2 × world.laneWidth), a 16 walkway each side, parapets (world.parapetWidth): as wide as a ground road and its pavements, 2 × (world.laneWidth + proto.shoulderWidth). P5.2. P3 and P4 keep 176 (hard shoulders 24).",
  "purpose": "The highway reads no wider than the street below it",
  "source": "Edit 41; Edit 42 (20 October); Edit 50.2 (22 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckPillarSpacing",
  "label": "Flyover pier spacing",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Column pairs, one under each deck edge, at this spacing. Never on the road or its pavement.",
  "purpose": "Piers are cover and sensor shadow on the ground",
  "source": "Edit 41; Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckInterval",
  "label": "Flyover traffic interval",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 2.4,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Seconds between deck cars per lane.",
  "purpose": "Busy but readable deck traffic",
  "source": "Edit 41 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.towerHeightMin",
  "label": "Tower height, lowest",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 288,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "The lowest a tower may be: eight storeys, above every Z2 roof.",
  "purpose": "Towers stand clear of the platform roofs and the high highway",
  "source": "Edit 41; Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.towerHeightMax",
  "label": "Tower height, highest",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 380,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "The highest a tower may be.",
  "purpose": "Tall but within the screen: 57 art px at 0.3 extrusion",
  "source": "Edit 41 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.roundaboutRadius",
  "label": "Roundabout radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of the Interchange roundabout.",
  "purpose": "Keeps the ring clear of the wider deck bands",
  "source": "Edit 41; Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.layout",
  "label": "Baked layout",
  "unit": "index",
  "default": 0,
  "min": -1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Which of the three baked layouts a city district plays. 0 = layout 1 in the sim; the game picks 1–3 at random each run; −1 generates live.",
  "purpose": "Playtest a layout by number",
  "source": "Edit 41 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.parapetWidth",
  "label": "Parapet width",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 8,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Deck parapet module, each side. The dummy stands inside it.",
  "purpose": "A barrier tile the dummy cannot cross except at a gap",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.jumpableHeight",
  "label": "Jumpable height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 20,
  "min": 8,
  "max": 40,
  "about": "Pieces tagged jumpable (terrain column jumpable) count at this height: handrails, parapets, crash and jersey barriers, low planters. A jump clears them above it.",
  "purpose": "Handrail height; below a sedan (19.8 at 0.9) plus margin",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.jumpableDepth",
  "label": "Jumpable depth",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 48,
  "min": 16,
  "max": 96,
  "about": "A Z0 piece is tagged jumpable only if, whole, it is no deeper than this across: one jump crosses it.",
  "purpose": "No landing inside a bed",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.jumpableTop",
  "label": "Jumpable art height",
  "unit": "art px",
  "default": 5,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 12,
  "about": "P6 art: a mass drawn no taller than this is a planter, a bed or reeds: tagged jumpable when no deeper than world.jumpableDepth. Edge planters and holds never are.",
  "purpose": "Planters and their hedges; not podiums or buildings",
  "source": "Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckLampSpacing",
  "label": "Deck lamp spacing",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 88,
  "min": 32,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Street lights stand in pairs, one on each parapet, every this many units: about nine per side across an 800-wide screen. Pale pole, bright head, a light pool on the walkway. Lamps beside a gap are down. P3 and P4 keep 128, staggered.",
  "purpose": "Reads as a highway; the downed lamps mark the drop points",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October); Edit 50.2 (22 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.stairLength",
  "label": "Stair length",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 64,
  "min": 32,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Run of a stair per world.deckHeight of rise, rounded to 16. Z0–Z1 64, Z0–Z2 112, Z1–Z2 48.",
  "purpose": "Two tiles: long enough to read as stairs",
  "source": "Edit 42; Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.stairWidth",
  "label": "Stair width",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 32,
  "min": 28,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Width of a stair and of a skybridge.",
  "purpose": "One tile; wider than the dummy (26)",
  "source": "Edit 42; Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.stairSpeed",
  "label": "Stair speed",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.7,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Ground speed multiplier on a stair. No dash on a stair.",
  "purpose": "Changing level costs time",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.dropSeconds",
  "label": "Drop time",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.35,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Fall from Z1 to the ground. A fall of height h takes × √(h / world.deckHeight): Z2 to the ground 0.47 s. No control, no contact until the landing.",
  "purpose": "A short hop: readable, not a dead stop",
  "source": "Edit 42; Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "damage.dropSpeed",
  "label": "Drop speed",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Downward speed at the landing after a Z1 fall; × √(h / world.deckHeight) for a fall of height h (Z2 to the ground about 161). Closing speed on a car is its hypotenuse with the car's speed across.",
  "purpose": "A landing on a stopped sedan scores 76; on a sedan at cruise, 176. The legs take it",
  "source": "Edit 42; Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.wallThickness",
  "label": "Perimeter wall",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 16,
  "min": 8,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Thickness of the wall on a district's outer edges (the river closes row 1).",
  "purpose": "Half a tile: the district is bounded",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.boomRange",
  "label": "Boom range",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 140,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A checkpoint boom lifts when a car on its level comes this close.",
  "purpose": "About a second at cruise: cars never stop",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.boomSeconds",
  "label": "Boom swing",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.5,
  "min": 0.05,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Time for a boom to swing fully up or down.",
  "purpose": "Up before the car arrives",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.wallHeight",
  "label": "Wall height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 40,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Drawn height of perimeter walls and checkpoint booths.",
  "purpose": "Lower than any building; reads as a boundary",
  "source": "Edit 42 (20 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckCrossAngle",
  "label": "Deck crossing angle",
  "unit": "degrees",
  "default": 5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 15,
  "about": "Within the deck band and one road half-width either side, a road under a flyover runs within this angle of square.",
  "purpose": "Flyovers always pass perpendicular to the road beneath",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckColumn",
  "label": "Deck column",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 24,
  "min": 8,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Side of a deck column footprint. Solid on the ground. P5.2: drawn round in section (a lit cylinder on an elliptical base, with a pier cap).",
  "purpose": "Wide enough to read as a column below the girder",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October); Edit 50.2 (22 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.turnRadiusTight",
  "label": "Tight turn radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 64,
  "min": 32,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of a tight corner, and of each corner of a stepped turn. P4 (Edit 49, proposed): the least bend on any road; no half-radius fallback; inner lane 36.",
  "purpose": "A city corner: the inner kerb nearly square",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.turnRadiusWide",
  "label": "Circular turn radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 224,
  "min": 64,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of a circular turn, where the straights allow it.",
  "purpose": "A sweeping bend: cars hold more speed",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.buildingSetback",
  "label": "Building setback",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 48,
  "min": 30,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Forecourt between the pavement and every building front. Spawns stand in it.",
  "purpose": "Buildings face the road at one line; the dummy fits beside the pavement",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.storeyHeight",
  "label": "Storey height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 36,
  "min": 12,
  "max": null,
  "about": "One storey, about 3 m. Buildings are 1 or 2 storeys, or towers of world.towerHeightMin–Max rounded to storeys.",
  "purpose": "Single, double and tower read apart by face and shadow",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.cornerAccel",
  "label": "Corner acceleration",
  "unit": "world units/s²",
  "default": 60,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Lateral limit in a bend: speed ≤ √(this × radius), braked for at traffic.comfortBraking.",
  "purpose": "Cars slow for tight corners (about 50 at the tight radius): a target",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.beamLength",
  "label": "Headlight reach",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 200,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Headlight beams follow the route ahead for this distance. They reach the screen before the car.",
  "purpose": "About 1.4 s of warning at cruise; objects in a beam cast shadows",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.beamAlpha",
  "label": "Headlight strength",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.22,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 0.6,
  "about": "Beam colour-math strength near the car, in three falloff bands. Day palette × 0.45.",
  "purpose": "Visible on dark ground, never brighter than a cue",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.brakeLength",
  "label": "Brake glow length",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 48,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Red wash on the ground behind a braking car.",
  "purpose": "A braking car reads from behind, not only by its lamps",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.brakeAlpha",
  "label": "Brake glow strength",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.35,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 0.6,
  "about": "Strength of the brake wash nearest the car, in three bands.",
  "purpose": "A soft wash: the warning stays the only solid red",
  "source": "Edit 43 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.highHeight",
  "label": "High level height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 216,
  "min": 160,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Height of Z2: the high highway, platform roofs and skybridges, about 18 m. Draws lifted 32 art px.",
  "purpose": "Clearly above the low highway; six storeys",
  "source": "Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.skybridgeMax",

  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 96,

  "purpose": "Spans an alley or a service lane, never a road",

 },
 {

 },
 {

  "purpose": "A roof walk, not a second street",

 },
 {
  "id": "world.skybridgeCount",

  "about": "Most skybridges on one screen, counting those between rows.",
  "purpose": "Keeps the ground readable under Z2",
  "source": "Edit 44 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckTopClear",
  "label": "Flyover top clearance",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 128,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Least distance from the top screen edge to a flyover as drawn: band top minus its lift (height × proto.art.extrusion). So a Z1 band starts at y ≥ 164, a Z2 band at y ≥ 192. Applies in every district.",
  "purpose": "Four 16 px tiles: a dummy entering from above shows before the deck hides it",
  "source": "Edit 48 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckBottomClear",
  "label": "Flyover bottom clearance",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 128,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Least ground between a flyover band and the bottom screen edge: band bottom at y ≤ 512.",
  "purpose": "Room for a row of buildings below the deck",
  "source": "Edit 48 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.edgeOverlap",
  "label": "Shared-edge overlap",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 32,
  "min": 32,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A building at a shared screen edge reaches at least this far into each screen. Up to 64 into the neighbour where it has room; otherwise the lot keeps world.pedestrianGap off the edge.",
  "purpose": "One 16 px tile: the building shows on both screens",
  "source": "Edit 48 (21 October)"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.followRoad",
  "label": "Follow along the lane",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "A car judges another lane's car only where it stands on this car's own lane ahead (1). 0: a straight strip ahead of the nose, as in P3, which points into the oncoming lane on a bend. The dummy sensor is unchanged.",
  "purpose": "No false head-on stops on bends; no mutual stop between opposing cars",
  "source": "Edit 49 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.gridlockSeconds",
  "label": "Jam hold",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 6,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "No new car enters a level while one of its cars has been stopped this long and its wait leads, car by car, to a cycle or to nothing. A queue behind the dummy or a cone does not count. 0 turns the hold off.",
  "purpose": "A jam never grows (user-directed hold; value proposed)",
  "source": "Edit 49 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.offscreenCullSeconds",
  "label": "Off-screen jam cleanup",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 12,
  "min": 2,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A car stopped this long on a screen out of view is removed (P6.1 managed traffic). Cars in view never vanish.",
  "purpose": "No jam survives out of sight",
  "source": "Edit 63 (26 October), P6.1"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.roundaboutEntryRadius",
  "label": "Roundabout entry radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 96,
  "min": 64,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of the fillet between a radial road and the ring, at both ends of the route round. Inner lane = this less half a lane.",
  "purpose": "Inner lane 68 instead of P3's 4",
  "source": "Edit 49 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.junctionTurnRadius",
  "label": "Junction turn radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 96,
  "min": 64,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of every turn at a priority junction (T, 45° Y, fork). Inner lane = this less half a lane; inner kerb = this less a lane.",
  "purpose": "The 96 bus stays within 3 of its lane through a junction turn; at 64 it swings 9 into the next lane",
  "source": "Edit 49.3 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.junctionApproach",
  "label": "Junction approach run",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 192,
  "min": 96,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A 45° road squared off to meet a road at 90°: the straight run before the junction, on the centre line. Its 45° bend uses world.turnRadiusWide. Lengthens to fit the turn radius.",
  "purpose": "Six tiles of square approach, so a 45° road meets the junction as a T",
  "source": "Edit 49.3 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.bus.swerve",
  "label": "Bus swerves",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "0: a bus only brakes for a hazard and never steers round it (canon: \"Only brakes; cannot swerve\"). 1: it swerves like the other classes.",
  "purpose": "Reads as a bus: a slow wall that stops",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "vehicles.bus.minRadius",
  "label": "Bus road radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 64,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A bus runs a ground road only if every bend of its centre line on screen keeps this radius (10% polyline tolerance), and never round a ring. Highways (straight) always carry buses.",
  "purpose": "The 96 bus stays in its lane: 0 outside at 160, 10 at 64",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.roundaboutArmGap",
  "label": "Roundabout arm gap",
  "unit": "deg",
  "default": 10,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Least ring arc left between two entry fillets where adjacent arms are close (a 45° arm). The fillets shrink to fit, never below world.turnRadiusTight.",
  "purpose": "A 45° arm beside a 90° arm without a cusp in the short turn",
  "source": "Edit 49.3 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckRingRadius",
  "label": "Elevated ring radius (Z1)",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 96,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of a Z1 elevated roundabout (Parkland). Highway arms join it on fillets of world.roundaboutEntryRadius. One way round, anticlockwise on screen. Edit 53.1: 132 → 200 (user, 25 October: \"widen the ring so highways can carry more\"). Outer lane 228, inner 172. Edit 55.2: 200 → 160 (user, 25 October: \"centred on the screen and slightly smaller\"). Outer lane 188, inner 132.",
  "purpose": "A ring with a visible hole at the 160 deck width, clear of the top edge",
  "source": "Edit 51 (22 October), P5 cityscape mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.deckRingRadiusHigh",
  "label": "Elevated ring radius (Z2)",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 96,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Centre-line radius of a Z2 elevated roundabout. Smaller than Z1: the Z2 lift needs more room under world.deckTopClear.",
  "purpose": "Fits an 800 × 640 screen with its 32 px lift",
  "source": "Edit 51 (22 October), P5 cityscape mock-up; retired in Edit 51.1"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.walkwayRingRadius",
  "label": "Walkway ring radius",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 112,
  "min": 64,
  "max": null,

  "purpose": "The monument stands clear inside it",

 },
 {

  "default": 28,
  "min": 20,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Width of a pedestrian walkway or bridge, parapets included. No traffic.",
  "purpose": "Narrower than a highway: reads as a footbridge",
  "source": "Edit 51 (22 October), P5 cityscape mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.walkwayRingHalf",

  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 168,
  "min": 96,

  "source": "Edit 51.1 (22 October), P5 district mock-up; 168 in Edit 51.2"
 },
 {

  "source": "Edit 51.1 (22 October), P5 district mock-up"
 },
 {

  "source": "Edit 51.1 (22 October), P5 district mock-up; redefined in Edit 51.2"
 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

 {
