```json
  "purpose": "The busy middle",
  "source": "Edit 51 (22 October), P5 cityscape mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.district.waterfront.quota",
  "label": "Waterfront quota",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Write-offs required in Waterfront (district 2 of 3; replaces Circus in P6).",
  "purpose": "The busy middle, by the river",
  "source": "Edit 62 (26 October), P6 game"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.district.monument.quota",
  "label": "Monument quota",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 4,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Write-offs required in Monument (district 3 of 3).",
  "purpose": "The finale",
  "source": "Edit 51 (22 October), P5 cityscape mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.diagonalShare",
  "label": "45° road share",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.25,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Chance that a city screen takes the diagonal style: a through-screen jog as one 45° segment, a corner cut at 45°. Never under a flyover.",
  "purpose": "45° roads occasionally (user-directed); value proposed",
  "source": "Edit 49 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.jamReverse",
  "label": "Jam reverse",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "A car jammed for traffic.gridlockSeconds backs up proto.unstick.reverseGap when the way behind is clear: a car on the wrong side of the road (a pass that met oncoming traffic), a stopped car behind it to make room, or the later of two cars facing each other. 0: P3, no reverse out of a jam.",
  "purpose": "Vehicles can reverse out of a pile-up",
  "source": "Edit 49 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.giveWayGap",
  "label": "Give-way gap",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 2.5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "A car stops at its give-way line while a car with priority would reach the shared span within this many seconds, or is in it. Roundabout: circulating cars have priority. Junction (Edit 49.3): the major road, then a left turn off it, then the minor road; the wait also covers the time to clear from a standstill at traffic.acceleration, plus perception.reactionDelay. Cars with priority let in a car that has crossed its line. 0: no give-way (P3).",
  "purpose": "Three- and four-arm roundabouts merge without cars driving through each other",
  "source": "Edit 49.1 (21 October), extended in Edit 49.3; P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.giveWaySpeed",
  "label": "Give-way approach speed",
  "unit": "world units/s",
  "default": 40,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "A car approaching a give-way line slows so that it could stop at the line, reaching it at no more than this speed.",
  "purpose": "A car can still stop when a gap closes late",
  "source": "Edit 49.1 (21 October), P4 test prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringOrbit",
  "label": "Ring orbit lane",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "On (1): a circulating car whose exit is blocked, or that is stuck behind a stopped or crawling car, moves to the inner ring lane and orbits until its exit and the outer lane beside it are clear. Off (0): the inner lane stays empty.",
  "purpose": "Uses the inner lane, as on a real roundabout; a blocked exit no longer stops the whole ring",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringLaneChange",
  "label": "Ring lane change length",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 150,
  "min": 40,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Distance a car travels while moving between the outer and inner ring lanes (one lane width, world.laneWidth). Its heading swings at most atan(π/2 × world.laneWidth ÷ this): 30° at 150. P4–P5 builds keep 72 (51°).",
  "purpose": "Cars change ring lanes in a gentle arc, not a swerve",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up; Edit 71 (27 October): 72 → 150 in P6"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringExitCheck",
  "label": "Ring exit check",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 120,
  "min": 48,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Length of exit road checked for a stopped car or the dummy. Something there means the exit is blocked: orbit, or keep orbiting.",
  "purpose": "Two car lengths past the point where the exit leaves the ring",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringMergeGap",
  "label": "Ring merge gap",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.2,
  "min": 0.5,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Seconds of closing speed a car needs behind it on the lane it moves into, on top of the two bodies and a margin.",
  "purpose": "A faster car behind is never cut off",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringPassRatio",
  "label": "Ring pass ratio",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.4,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A circulating car may pass on the inner lane when the car ahead on the outer lane goes slower than this share of its cruise speed.",
  "purpose": "Passing only round a stopped or crawling car",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringMaxLaps",
  "label": "Ring laps",
  "unit": "laps",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Lap paths built per ring route. Edit 53.1: past the last, the last lap repeats; a car orbits until its exit and the outer lane are clear (no last-lap wait).",
  "purpose": "Long enough for most blocks to clear; never endless",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.roundaboutMinArms",
  "label": "Roundabout arms",
  "unit": "arms",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 3,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Every roundabout, ground or elevated, has at least this many arms.",
  "purpose": "A ring with two arms is a bend: nobody gives way",
  "source": "Edit 52 (24 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.deckGapScale",
  "label": "Highway spacing scale",
  "unit": "×",
  "default": 1.5,
  "min": 0.5,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Deck admissions per lane every world.deckInterval times this.",
  "purpose": "Fewer cars on the highway: about half as many on screen at 1.4× speed",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.deckSpeedScale",
  "label": "Highway speed scale",
  "unit": "×",
  "default": 1.4,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Deck (highway) cars cruise at their class cruise speed times this.",
  "purpose": "Highways run faster: harder hits, less time to read a car",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.share.bus",
  "label": "Bus share",
  "unit": "share",
  "default": 0.1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of scheduled traffic that is buses. A bus slot on a screen that fails vehicles.bus.minRadius runs as a sedan.",
  "purpose": "Buses where the road suits them",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.share.police",
  "label": "Police share",
  "unit": "share",
  "default": 0.08,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of scheduled traffic that is police (Warden).",
  "purpose": "Rare enough to notice",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.share.hatchback",
  "label": "Hatchback share",
  "unit": "share",
  "default": 0.22,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Share of a district's scheduled traffic that is hatchbacks. Vans take the district's vanShare first; sedans take what the shares leave.",
  "purpose": "The light, quick model of the five",
  "source": "Edit 50 (21 October), P5 prototype"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.continuous",
  "label": "District-wide traffic",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "On (1): every screen of a district runs its traffic every tick. A car crossing a shared edge moves to the matching lane next door with its id, model, speed, offset and plan. Cars within world.spawnLeadIn of an edge are seen and drawn next door. The shift's cars enter only where a road or highway crosses the district boundary; at the start every lane runs at its steady headway. Off (0): P5.2, one screen at a time.",
  "purpose": "A car followed across a screen edge is the same car at the same speed (user decision, 25 October)",
  "source": "Edit 53 (25 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.warmSeconds",
  "label": "District warm-up",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 30,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "The district mock-up runs its linked traffic this long before it is shown, so every screen is already busy.",
  "purpose": "No screen waits for cars (mock-up only)",
  "source": "Edit 53 (25 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.exitAfterSeams",
  "label": "Way out after",
  "unit": "seams",
  "default": 9,
  "min": 1,
  "max": null,
  "about": "In the district mock-up a car picks its route afresh at every seam. After this many seams it takes the route with fewest seams to a district exit.",
  "purpose": "No car circles a closed street for ever; the district never fills (mock-up only)",
  "source": "Edit 53 (25 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.district.parkland.interval",
  "label": "Parkland traffic interval",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 3.2,
  "min": 0.5,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Each lane entering Parkland (road or highway) admits one car per 2 × this. The four highway entries all feed the Z1 ring.",

  "source": "Edit 53 (25 October), P5 sim and district mock-up; Edit 63 (26 October): 3.2, as Waterfront (equal flow)"
 },
 {

  "purpose": "Busier than Parkland (mock-up)",
  "source": "Edit 53 (25 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {

 },
 {

  "purpose": "Steady flow on the one snake road (mock-up)",
  "source": "Edit 53 (25 October), P5 sim and district mock-up"
 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

 },

 },
 {

  "source": "Edit 53.1 (25 October)"
 },
 {

  "purpose": "Cars turning 90° or 180° keep the outer lane (user, 25 October)",
  "source": "Edit 54 (25 October)"
 },
 {

  "unit": "cars",

  "min": 0,

  "source": "Edit 56 (26 October)"
 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

 },
 {

 },

 {

 {

 {
  "id": "proto.headingBlend",
  "label": "Heading blend",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 12,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A car's heading blends from one path segment's tangent to the next within this distance of a vertex (at most half of either segment). The heading turns continuously; the sprite shows the nearest of its 32 headings. 0 restores the stepped heading.",
  "purpose": "Cars turn smoothly round rings and fillets",
  "source": "Edit 71 (27 October): Prototype 6.3 driving"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.ringEntryGap",
  "label": "Roundabout entry gap",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 2.0,
  "min": 0.5,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A car at a roundabout's give-way line waits while a circulating car would reach its join within this many seconds. Half a second under traffic.giveWayGap (junctions keep that). P6 only (Edit 72): the shared sim behind the lab pieces keeps 6.2's rings (the junction gap; no turn signals).",
  "purpose": "Entering cars take the gaps a real driver takes",
  "source": "Edit 71 (27 October): Prototype 6.3 driving"
 },
 {
  "id": "traffic.signalDistance",
  "label": "Turn signal distance",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 140,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A car signals a turn from this far before it. Junction: toward the turn, until it ends. Roundabout (Edit 72, the Highway Code's rule 186 mirrored for right-hand traffic): from this far before the line, right for the first exit, left past 12 o'clock or for a U-turn, none straight on, held onto the ring; toward the exit once past the exit before its own or moving out of the inner lane, held until traffic.signalCancel past the exit. P6 only (Edit 72): the shared sim behind the lab pieces keeps 6.2's rings (the junction gap; no turn signals).",
  "purpose": "Players and drivers read a car's turn",
  "source": "Edit 71 (27 October): Prototype 6.3 driving; Edit 72: roundabout signals held steady"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.droneConeSeconds",
  "label": "Cone drone delay",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 5,
  "min": 0,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A cone left in a lane this long fetches a FARSIGHT drone, whether cars stop or steer round it. 0: only a car blocked and honking calls one (P2–P5).",
  "purpose": "No cone stays in the road for long",
  "source": "Edit 71 (27 October): Prototype 6.3 driving"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.signalLength",
  "label": "Turn signal length",
  "unit": "art px",
  "default": 4,
  "min": 2,
  "max": null,
  "about": "Each turn signal light: 2 art px wide, this many art px along the car from each corner on the signalling side. Front and rear blink together at 1.5 Hz in amber (K.amber). Was 2.",
  "purpose": "A turn signal reads at play scale",
  "source": "Edit 71 (27 October): Prototype 6.3 driving"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.coneHeight",
  "label": "Cone height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 16,
  "min": 14,
  "max": null,
  "about": "A cone's drawn height: its sprite stands this ÷ U (2) art px; its shadow falls from this height. P1–6.2: 14 (7 art px). Edit 71: 16 (8 art px; 14% taller, the nearest whole pixel to 15%).",
  "purpose": "Cones read at a glance",
  "source": "P1 (Prototype Log, Art: extrusion heights); registered Edit 71 (27 October): 14 → 16"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.dummyHeight",
  "label": "Dummy height",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 18,
  "min": 14,
  "max": null,
  "about": "The dummy's standing height: drawn, and (10.6) where sightlines to its top run. Registered in GDD Edit 106 at its sim.js value",
  "purpose": "The dummy's standing height: drawn, and (10.6) where sightlines to its top run",
  "source": "Registered in GDD Edit 106 (Prototype 10.6) at the value in prototypes/p6/sim.js"
 },
 {

 },
 {

  "purpose": "A snap, then a glide",

 },
 {
  "id": "proto.turnModel",
  "label": "Turn model",
  "unit": "enum 1–3",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "How the dummy turns. 1 LAG (P2–6.4): moves at once, facing follows at movement.turnRate, slowed while misaligned, lunge along facing. 2 DIRECT: facing follows the input at movement.directTurnRate, lunge where the player points. 3 PIVOT: plants and pivots past movement.pivotAngle at movement.pivotRate; lunge along facing. Decided 27 October: DIRECT (P6.5). The shared sim keeps 1 for P2–6.4.",
  "purpose": "Test which turn reads right on a d-pad, a stick and keys",
  "source": "Edit 73 (27 October): controls prototype; Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "movement.directTurnRate",
  "label": "Direct turn rate",
  "unit": "degrees/s",
  "default": 1080,
  "min": 180,
  "max": null,
  "about": "DIRECT: facing turns toward the input or aim at this rate. The lunge does not wait for it.",
  "purpose": "The body shows the turn; the input decides the lunge",
  "source": "Edit 73 (27 October): controls prototype; Edit 75 (27 October): Prototype 6.5"
 },
 {
  "id": "movement.pivotAngle",
  "label": "Pivot angle",
  "unit": "°",
  "default": 90,
  "min": 30,
  "max": 180,
  "about": "PIVOT: a turn sharper than this plants the dummy until it faces within movement.alignThreshold.",
  "purpose": "Sharp turns read as a plant, not a wander",
  "source": "Edit 73 (27 October): controls prototype"
 },
 {

  "about": "PIVOT: facing turns at this rate, planted or on the move.",
  "purpose": "A plant lasts a beat",
  "source": "Edit 73 (27 October): controls prototype"
 },
 {

 {
  "id": "proto.bounce.maxThrow",
  "label": "Longest throw",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 40,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 400,
  "about": "A knock off a car slides the dummy at most this far: its speed is capped at √(2 × slide deceleration × this). 0: no cap (P6.8–7.1).",
  "purpose": "\"The initial ricochet is far too strong\": the dummy takes damage, so it keeps less of the momentum",
  "source": "Edit 83 (28 October): Prototype 7.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.ferryDepth",
  "label": "Ferry drop depth",
  "unit": "world units",
  "default": 160,
  "min": 20,
  "max": 400,
  "about": "After a clear the drone carries the dummy into the next tile and sets it down off the road, within this of the entry edge (nearest the crossing).",
  "purpose": "\"deposits the player on a part of the screen close to the entrance point but not on the road\"",
  "source": "Edit 83 (28 October): Prototype 7.2"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.saveSlots",
  "label": "Story save slots",
  "unit": "slots",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 4,
  "about": "STORY opens the save screen: NEW GAME, then this many slots. Each keeps a name, a time stamp and one story's progress (district, tile in test, cleared tiles, points, credits, levels, chassis). Saved in this browser.",
  "purpose": "Several players, or several runs, on one device (user request, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 88 (28 October): Prototype 7.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.saveNameMax",
  "label": "Save name length",
  "unit": "characters",
  "default": 12,
  "min": 4,
  "max": 16,
  "about": "A slot's name holds at most this many characters: A–Z, 0–9, space, - . ' (upper case). The default name is TEST DUMMY <slot>.",
  "purpose": "A 12-character file name (user request, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 88 (28 October): Prototype 7.7"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.level.firstPoints",
  "label": "Points to level 2",
  "unit": "points",
  "default": 6500,
  "min": 1000,
  "max": 50000,
  "about": "Story: points level the dummy up. Level 2 needs this many points; each next level needs story.level.stepPoints more than the one before (levels 2–9 at 6,500, 15,000, 25,500, 38,000, 52,500, 69,000, 87,500, 108,000).",
  "purpose": "A first level-up within two bodies: the hook (user request, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 89 (28 October): Prototype 7.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.level.stepPoints",
  "label": "Points per level, growth",
  "unit": "points",
  "default": 2000,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 20000,
  "about": "Each level needs this many more points than the last. At 3,500 points a body the last level lands in Waterfront.",
  "purpose": "Levels slow as the story goes on; the tracks fill near the end",
  "source": "Edit 89 (28 October): Prototype 7.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.level.max",
  "label": "Top level",
  "unit": "level",
  "default": 9,
  "min": 2,
  "max": 20,
  "about": "The dummy's top level. Levels 2 to this one each grant story.level.upgrades upgrades: 8, one for each level the drones do not bring (Battery 1–3, Legs 2–3, Hands 2, Arms 2–3).",
  "purpose": "One upgrade for every level that is not a verb",
  "source": "Edit 89 (28 October): Prototype 7.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.level.upgrades",
  "label": "Upgrades per level",
  "unit": "upgrades",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "Upgrades a level-up grants. An upgrade fits the next level of an open track in the workshop (Battery from the start; Legs, Hands, Arms once their verb arrives).",
  "purpose": "Story spends upgrades, not credits (user request, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 89 (28 October): Prototype 7.8"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.giftEvery",
  "label": "Arcade gift interval",
  "unit": "write-offs",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "Arcade: Farsight's drone issues the next verb (Legs 1, Hands 1, Arms 1, the story's order) after every this many write-offs.",
  "purpose": "\"ARCADE and STORY need the same kind of mechanic\"",
  "source": "Edit 84 (28 October): Prototype 7.3"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.gift.fallSeconds",
  "label": "Gift fall",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0.7,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 3,
  "about": "The new module falls onto the dummy's body in the gift screen over this time; then the flash and fanfare.",
  "purpose": "\"an animation of the new item arriving\"",
  "source": "Edit 84 (28 October): Prototype 7.3"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.gift.holdSeconds",
  "label": "Gift hold",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "The gift screen ignores A for this long, so a mashed button cannot skip the moment.",
  "purpose": "\"JUICE and SATISFACTION\"",
  "source": "Edit 84 (28 October): Prototype 7.3"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.tip.holdSeconds",
  "label": "Field note hold",
  "unit": "seconds",
  "default": 7,
  "min": 2,
  "max": 20,
  "about": "A one-time FARSIGHT field note (a tip, shown once per save file) stays on screen this long, with its timer bar. Prototype 10.8: the lure's notes",
  "purpose": "A one-time FARSIGHT field note (a tip, shown once per save file) stays on screen this long, with its timer bar. Prototype 10.8: the lure's notes",
  "source": "GDD Edit 108 amendment (Prototype 10.8): DECISIONS row 729"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.finale.unveilSeconds",
  "label": "Unveiling",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 1.2,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 5,
  "about": "Story, the finale: a Farsight drone lifts the cloth off the circus's monument over this time; then the plaque fades in and A goes on to the ship. Reduced motion: no lift.",
  "purpose": "The unveiling is seen: the cloth leaves before the plaque reads. 1.2 s, the gift screen's hold (ui.gift.holdSeconds)",
  "source": "Edit 94 (P7.13): added in manual review of the coding agent's R-UNVEIL (D76)"
 },
 {

  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a highway with no planned drop breaks its parapet once over each Z0 road it crosses: one world.tileSize module, centred where the road's centre line meets the parapet; south on an east–west deck, east on a north–south one (the other side where a flight's head stands). Drawn lit: white snapped ends, a cyan edge. 6.x builds run 0.",
  "purpose": "Every highway is a place to drop onto cars (user rule, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 85 (28 October): Prototype 7.4"
 },
 {

  "label": "Highway flights, minimum",
  "unit": "flights",

  "min": 0,
  "max": 2,

  "source": "Edit 85 (28 October): Prototype 7.4"
 },
 {

  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "A cone's home shows at least this share of the cone's pixels: never under a highway, ring or walkway as drawn, never behind a building's face. Homes stay on the pavement beside a road, never on it. 6.x builds run 0 (no test).",
  "purpose": "A cone the player cannot see is no bait (user rule, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 85 (28 October): Prototype 7.4"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.batteryModel",
  "label": "Battery model",
  "unit": "mode",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "0: 6.x (the dash from the start; Battery levels speed the recharge). 1: Edit 91 (Battery 1 is the dash; Battery 2 a second charge, to battery.capacity; Battery 3 the faster recharge, abilities.battery.level3Seconds). P7 runs 1.",
  "purpose": "\"The first powerup should be given after the first screen … Battery 1 = Dash\" (user, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 91 (28 October): Prototype 7.10"
 },
 {

  "purpose": "Battery 2 is the second charge",
  "source": "Edit 91 (28 October): Prototype 7.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "damage.bodyHealthOnFoot",

  "unit": "health",
  "default": 500,
  "min": 100,
  "max": 2000,
  "about": "Model 1: the chassis's health while the dash is not fitted (Story, Monument A1). With Battery 1 fitted, damage.bodyHealth; a fit mid-chassis adds the difference.",
  "purpose": "\"In Screen 1, the Dummy's health is only 500 (since they cannot dash)\" (user, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 91 (28 October): Prototype 7.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "story.missFloor",
  "label": "Miss floor",
  "unit": "health",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 50,
  "about": "Story, the tile in test: a hit that would write the chassis off but misses the brief leaves this much health. The chassis plays on; only a hit to the brief writes it off. 0 turns it off.",
  "purpose": "\"If the FINAL hit does NOT kill the player, then they can move around at 1 health\" (user, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 91 (28 October): Prototype 7.10"
 },
 {

  "label": "Dummy monument",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the DUMMY MONUMENT stands on the circus's island (Waterfront B2): the dummy in white stone, three times life, on a stepped pedestal with a lit plaque. 6.x worlds run 0.",
  "purpose": "\"In the centre of the circus there must be a kind of DUMMY MONUMENT\" (user, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 91 (28 October): Prototype 7.10"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.storyArt",
  "label": "Story tile art",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the story's tile art (Narrative A): Monument A1's welcome wall, A2's shop signs, A3's lit cubes, C3's gateway; Parkland C1's lantern, B1's falls and runnels; Waterfront's customs signs (A3, C3) and the ship at B3. Cosmetic only: no solid, floor, stair, spawn or cone home changes. 6.x worlds run 0.",
  "purpose": "\"Update the tile names, and tile assets (cosmetic only without changing gameplay interaction elements)\" (user, 28 October)",
  "source": "Edit 92 (28 October): Prototype 7.11"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.monumentShroud",
  "label": "Monument shroud",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "P7: the DUMMY MONUMENT in Waterfront B2 is shrouded in Story until Waterfront B2 clears (Edit 92 §3.2, D76)",
  "purpose": "Edit 94 (P7.13): the finale's reward is seen; the circus's monument stays covered until the last tile clears. 1 in P7SIM; 0 keeps 6.10 and older rules",
  "source": "Edit 94 (P7.13): written by the coding agent (coder/, run 20261029-105801); group and owner aligned in manual review"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.grades",
  "label": "Arcade grades",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Arcade runs the shift in grades: no end at the quota; a grade every world.district.<id>.quota write-offs; the run ends when the shift's cars run out (Licensed after one full grade, else Failed test), or on a refusal. 0 keeps Prototype 7's quota ending; Prototype 8.2 sets 1",
  "purpose": "Edit 97: a score run for the high scores; things get harder after every X write-offs",
  "source": "Edit 97 (P8.2): user-decided, 29 October: \"Maybe your body gets more damage to destroy, and you get denser or faster cars\"; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.grade.healthStep",
  "label": "Grade: chassis health",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.25,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Each grade past 1 adds this share of the base chassis health (damage.bodyHealth at the chosen difficulty) to the next chassis",
  "purpose": "\"Your body gets more damage to destroy\"",
  "source": "Edit 97 (P8.2): user-decided, 29 October: \"Maybe your body gets more damage to destroy, and you get denser or faster cars\"; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.grade.densityStep",
  "label": "Grade: traffic density",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Each grade past 1 adds this share to the district's traffic density on every tile (the Edit 85 density hook)",
  "purpose": "\"Denser cars\". Faster cars not built (D81): cruise ×1.2 with density ×1.5 lowered the mean speed in a headless test",
  "source": "Edit 97 (P8.2): user-decided, 29 October: \"Maybe your body gets more damage to destroy, and you get denser or faster cars\"; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.grade.max",
  "label": "Top grade",
  "unit": "grade",
  "default": 5,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "The highest grade. Past it, health and traffic stay as they are",
  "purpose": "Grade 5: chassis health ×2, traffic density ×1.8 at the defaults",
  "source": "Edit 97 (P8.2): user-decided, 29 October: \"Maybe your body gets more damage to destroy, and you get denser or faster cars\"; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "highscore.tableSize",
  "label": "High score table size",
  "unit": "entries",
  "default": 10,
  "min": 3,
  "max": 50,
  "about": "Scores kept per table, in this browser: one table per Arcade district, one for Story's Certified runs",
  "purpose": "Edit 97: HIGH SCORES under the two modes (user-decided: this browser)",
  "source": "Edit 97 (P8.2): user-decided, 29 October: \"Maybe your body gets more damage to destroy, and you get denser or faster cars\"; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.introScreen",
  "label": "Intro screen",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the game opens on a full-screen intro: DUMMIES, the front-on animation (the dummy steps into the road, a car comes on), one line (n36) and START; then the mode select, STORY first. 0: the mode select first. Prototype 8.3 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"Create a NEW Introduction Screen - Dummies (full screen) + Start\"",
  "source": "Edit 98 (P8.3): user-decided, 29 October (the intro screen, the level card, the drone drop, the Story animation); values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.levelCard",
  "label": "Level card",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a level starts on a full-screen card in both modes: ACT I: MONUMENT. Tile A1. Level 1 of 27. The tale. >>> The goal. Arcade: ARCADE: PARKLAND. Grade 1 of 5. The district's line. >>> The goal (n37). Prototype 8.3 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"An Exciting Screen that tells you very simply what the goal of the level is\"",
  "source": "Edit 98 (P8.3): user-decided, 29 October (the intro screen, the level card, the drone drop, the Story animation); values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "world.droneDropIn",
  "label": "Drone drop",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: after the level card, a drone carries the dummy in from the screen's left edge and sets it down where it would have stood (proto.droneSpeed, proto.droneLiftSeconds). A Story tile deployed from the map; an Arcade district. Prototype 8.3 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"You are dropped off from the left side of a screen by a DRONE\"",
  "source": "Edit 98 (P8.3): user-decided, 29 October (the intro screen, the level card, the drone drop, the Story animation); values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.storySplash",
  "label": "Story splash",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: NEW GAME or CONTINUE plays a full-screen animation before the map: the dummy's six parts fly apart and back, in the gift screen's style. Prototype 8.3 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"An animation showing the different body parts flying\"",
  "source": "Edit 98 (P8.3): user-decided, 29 October (the intro screen, the level card, the drone drop, the Story animation); values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.intro.loopSeconds",
  "label": "Intro loop",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 6.5,
  "min": 2,
  "max": 20,
  "about": "One loop of the intro animation: the dummy steps out, the car comes on, the hit, the fade",
  "purpose": "Long enough to read the line twice",
  "source": "Edit 98 (P8.3): user-decided, 29 October (the intro screen, the level card, the drone drop, the Story animation); values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.splash.cycleSeconds",
  "label": "Story splash cycle",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 3.6,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "One cycle of the Story splash: assembled, the parts fly out, hold, fly back",
  "purpose": "The gift screen's pace",
  "source": "Edit 98 (P8.3): user-decided, 29 October (the intro screen, the level card, the drone drop, the Story animation); values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.driver.coneSteepPass",
  "label": "Steep pass at a cone",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a car slower than proto.driver.passSpeed at a cone pulls out steeply, as a pass round the dummy does (clamp(1.1 × gap, 40, proto.swerveLength)). A car stopped at a cone steers round it once the way is clear. 0: P2 to 8.3, the full proto.swerveLength; a stopped car waits for the drone. Prototype 8.4 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"cars do not divert around cones, they just wait there until the drone comes and takes away the cone\"",
  "source": "Edit 99 (P8.4): user-reported errors, 29 October; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.beamStraight",
  "label": "Straight beams",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: headlight beams run straight ahead from the nose along the heading for proto.art.beamLength, as the forward sensor sees (proto.sensorStraight). 0: they follow the route. Prototype 8.4 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"the rays of headlights also bend with the streets instead of going straight ahead\"",
  "source": "Edit 99 (P8.4): user-reported errors, 29 October; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.noticeFlashHz",
  "label": "Notice flash",
  "unit": "Hz",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 10,
  "about": "A car that has noticed the dummy (in its caution zone for perception.reactionDelay) flashes its headlamps and beam at this rate. 0: no flash; the intention chevron instead (P3 to 8.3). Prototype 8.4: 4",
  "purpose": "\"Has noticed you should have flashing headlights\"",
  "source": "Edit 99 (P8.4): user-reported errors, 29 October; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.brakeGlowStopped",
  "label": "Brake lights held",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a car braking for safety, or holding a planned stop, keeps its brake lights and ground glow at a standstill. 0: the glow only while moving. Prototype 8.4 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"Braking for safety should also have brake lights on\"",
  "source": "Edit 99 (P8.4): user-reported errors, 29 October; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.art.spentGrey",
  "label": "Spent car grey",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a car that has hit the dummy (spent) draws with a grey outline and its body dimmed toward grey. 0: a slash on the roof. Prototype 8.4 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"when you have hit a car once already, the car should clearly not be available, perhaps the outline can turn from blue to grey\"",
  "source": "Edit 99 (P8.4): user-reported errors, 29 October; values proposed"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.cueKey.simple",
  "label": "Simple cue key",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the pause screen's cue key has seven rows (Has noticed you, Route fixed, Slowing, Braking for safety, Already hit you, Blocked warning you, What you can see); no chevron, triangle or slash on car roofs; no sensor-shadow outlines. 0: eleven rows (P3 to 8.3). Prototype 8.4 sets 1",
  "purpose": "\"there are too many CUE KEYS for the cars\"",
  "source": "Edit 99 (P8.4): user-reported errors, 29 October; values proposed"
 },
 {

 },

 {
  "id": "audio.sfx.spentRepeatMax",
  "label": "Spent repeats",
  "unit": "count",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 20,
  "about": "Knock and touch-spent off one spent car: each sounds this many times at most in any audio.sfx.spentRepeatSeconds. 0: no cap",
  "purpose": "Lawrence, after the QA run: leaning on a spent car knocked 57–74 times a minute (row 624)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's ruling, 30 October 11:30 (DECISIONS row 624)"
 },
 {
  "id": "audio.sfx.spentRepeatSeconds",
  "label": "Spent window",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 2,
  "min": 0.1,
  "max": 30,
  "about": "The window audio.sfx.spentRepeatMax counts in, sim seconds",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: 2 max within a 2 second window (row 624)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's ruling, 30 October 11:30 (DECISIONS row 624)"
 },
 {
  "id": "audio.sfx.dashDamagedAt",
  "label": "Dash: damaged",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.45,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Wear (1 − health ÷ maximum) from which the dash plays sfx.lunge.damaged; the dummy smokes from here",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: B2 when damaged (row 629)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's ruling, 30 October 11:30 (DECISIONS row 629)"
 },
 {
  "id": "audio.sfx.dashWreckedAt",
  "label": "Dash: very damaged",
  "unit": "ratio",
  "default": 0.7,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "Wear from which the dash plays sfx.lunge.wrecked; sparks and a cracked head from here",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: B3 when very damaged (row 629)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's ruling, 30 October 11:30 (DECISIONS row 629)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.contactGraceSeconds",
  "label": "Contact grace",
  "unit": "s",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 2,
  "about": "Seconds a dummy–car contact outlives its last touch. A touch on the same car within it continues the contact: no new bounce, hit, touch-spent or knock. 0: Prototype 8.4's rule (a contact ends the step it stops touching). Prototype 9 sets 0.3",
  "purpose": "Leaning on a stopped car bounced the dummy ~16 times a second (QA-02, row 630)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:11: 'if the dummy touches the same car again within about 0.3 s, treat it as the same collision, with no new bounce or hit' (DECISIONS row 630; QA-02, D88)"
 },
 {
  "id": "audio.music.track.cleared",
  "label": "Tile cleared track",
  "unit": "track",
  "default": 11,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 11,
  "about": "Track from a Story tile's clear to the next card: the write-off, the report, the ferry, the gift screen. Not ducked by them; pause ducks it",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: 'Tile Cleared' music once a tile is cleared (row 631)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's ruling, 30 October 11:30 (DECISIONS row 631)"
 },
 {
  "id": "proto.driver.dodgeFollowsCone",
  "label": "Dodge follows the cone",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a car that swerved round a cone returns to its lane once that cone is no longer where it stood (moved, carried, thrown or fetched), as soon as its commitment ends. A swerve already under way still finishes (Route locked). 0: Prototype 8.4's rule: it holds the other lane until it passes where the cone stood. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "Cars kept diverting round a cone that had moved (row 632)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's report, 31 October 16:29: 'I put down a cone ... then i moved the cone to another place. and the cars still divert around the original location' (DECISIONS row 632); the fix proposed by the assistant"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.lampsOnCar",
  "label": "Lamps on the car",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a car that has noticed the dummy flashes its lamps on the art's own headlights (the roof plane, at the front corners), in play and in the cue key; the glow stays on the road ahead. 0: Prototype 8.4's lamps, half a car's height lower and ahead of the bumper. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "The flash sits on the headlights (row 634)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:29: \"'has noticed you' icon is a bit weird cause the blinking headlights are not where the car headlights are\" (DECISIONS row 634)"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.pause.quiet",
  "label": "Quiet pause page",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: the pause page drops the gamepad monitor line and Story's save note. 0: Prototype 8.4's page. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "A clean pause page (row 635)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:29: the pause screen shows 'gamepad #0' bottom left; 'the story map saves this tile's count' maybe should not show (DECISIONS row 635)"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.conesXray",
  "label": "Cones x-ray",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: a cone on the ground or in flight is drawn again as a checker of its own pixels after the decks and overpasses, as the dummy and cars are (the x-ray pass), so a cone under a highway reads. 0: Prototype 8.4: a cone under a deck is hidden. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "Cones under decks read (row 637)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:40: 'cones also should be visible (like the dummy) under overpasses and the highway' (DECISIONS row 637)"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.modes",
  "label": "Arcade modes",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: Arcade asks for a mode above the district list: Time trial (arcade.trial.writeOffs, fastest wins) or Endless (the grades, arcade.endless.gradeWriteOffs a grade in every district). The high scores keep a fastest table per district and for Story. 0: Prototype 8.2–8.4's district quotas. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "A clear Arcade goal (row 639)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:40–16:5x: Arcade unclear; his choice: two modes, Time trial (10 write-offs, fastest) and Endless (one target everywhere) (DECISIONS row 639)"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.trial.writeOffs",
  "label": "Time trial target",
  "unit": "count",
  "default": 10,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 50,
  "about": "Time trial: the write-offs to finish. The clock runs in play only (not the card, report, workshop or pause); the cars running out first ends it Failed test",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: 'time trial, like 10 write offs' (row 639)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:40–16:5x: Arcade unclear; his choice: two modes, Time trial (10 write-offs, fastest) and Endless (one target everywhere) (DECISIONS row 639)"
 },
 {
  "id": "arcade.endless.gradeWriteOffs",
  "label": "Endless grade target",
  "unit": "count",
  "default": 3,
  "min": 1,
  "max": 20,
  "about": "Endless: write-offs per grade, the same in every district (replaces world.district.<id>.quota in Arcade)",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: 'each zone should have the same write-off requirement' (row 639)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:40–16:5x: Arcade unclear; his choice: two modes, Time trial (10 write-offs, fastest) and Endless (one target everywhere) (DECISIONS row 639)"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.exploreArrows",
  "label": "Exploration arrows",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: in Arcade, chevrons at each open stretch of the screen's edge point to the next screen, and the district map marks the screens next door you can walk to. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "Exploring the district reads (row 642)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 16:40: 'in arcade mode, how can it be clear that you can explore the city? ... maybe the map has arrows that show where you can move to next?' (DECISIONS row 642)"
 },
 {
  "id": "audio.music.trackPerUpgrade",
  "label": "Track per upgrade",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 1,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: each part fitted (a purchase in the workshop, a part by drone) moves the level music to the rotation's next track, until the next card. 0: the track holds from card to card (row 611)",
  "purpose": "Lawrence: 'the music should change with each upgrade you get' (row 643)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence's ruling, 30 October 11:30 (DECISIONS row 643)"
 },
 {
  "id": "ui.coreOnChest",
  "label": "Battery core on the chest",
  "unit": "flag",
  "default": 0,
  "min": 0,
  "max": 1,
  "about": "1: Battery 1 (fastCharge) draws on the chassis as a round, lit core at the centre of the chest, a chrome rim with four bolts; Battery 3's chest pack sits around it. 0: Prototype 7.x–8.4's charge port on the pelvis, its cable between the legs. Prototype 9 sets 1",
  "purpose": "The battery reads as the chassis's power, not its crotch (row 644)",
  "source": "Edit 100 (P9): Lawrence, 31 October 20:58: 'the position of the battery is too close to crotch. it should be in the centre of the chest like Iron Man's power core' (DECISIONS row 644)"
 },
```

```json
{
 "map": {

 },
 "tiles": [
  {
   "n": 1,
   "tile": "A1",
   "title": "The welcome wall",
   "stage": "10.1",
   "newIdea": "Controls: walk into the red colleague's path; it never brakes.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 2,
    "label": "TWO HITS BY RED"
   },
   "params": {},
   "tale": "Fresh off the factory floor. Your colleague has learned nothing yet. Neither have you. Make engineering proud.",
   "hint": "Walk into its lane. It will not stop."
  },
  {
   "n": 2,
   "tile": "B1",
   "title": "The podium bridge",
   "stage": "10.2",
   "newIdea": "After one crash it brakes for dummies in its lane.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 2,
    "label": "TWO HITS, ONE LESSON"
   },
   "params": {},
   "tale": "Battery 1 arrives when your colleague first brakes. It now stops for anyone in its lane. Arrive from the side.",
   "hint": "Once it brakes for you, dash in from beside the lane."
  },
  {
   "n": 3,
   "tile": "C1",
   "title": "The LED drum",
   "stage": "10.3",
   "newIdea": "Side sensors: it stops for a dummy waiting beside the road.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 3,
    "label": "THREE HITS BY RED"
   },
   "params": {
    "perception.sideNoticeSeconds": 0.8
   },
   "tale": "Side sensors fitted, per policy. Lingering at the kerb is now noticed. Legs 1 arrives; go over, not beside.",
   "hint": "Wait on the plinth, or jump in over the kerb."
  },
  {
   "n": 4,
   "tile": "C2",
   "title": "The portal tower",
   "stage": "10.4",
   "newIdea": "Roof lidar: it sees a dummy raised on the plinth.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 3,
    "label": "THREE HITS BY RED"
   },
   "params": {},
   "tale": "Head office approved roof lidar. The plinth no longer hides you. Height was a privilege; jumping is a right.",
   "hint": "Leave the plinth. Jump in from the gap or cover."
  },
  {
   "n": 5,
   "tile": "B2",
   "title": "The monument",
   "stage": "10.5",
   "newIdea": "Street cameras: it sees a dummy hiding behind cover.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 2,
    "label": "TWO HITS BY RED"
   },
   "params": {
    "duel.redReturnSeconds": 3
   },
   "tale": "The plaque reads ZERO ACCIDENTS. Street cameras now watch behind cover. Hands 1 arrives: a cone makes colleagues stop.",
   "hint": "Drop a cone in its lane, then dash as it swerves."
  },
  {
   "n": 6,
   "tile": "A2",
   "title": "The neon shops",
   "stage": "10.6",
   "newIdea": "DUCK: crouch behind low planters; it now stops for cones.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 2,
    "label": "TWO HITS BY RED"
   },
   "params": {
    "perception.duckNoticeFactor": 2
   },
   "tale": "The neon shops sell nothing. Your colleague stops for cones. DUCK arrives: the planters are low, and so are you.",
   "hint": "Duck behind a planter. Spring as it passes."
  },
  {
   "n": 7,
   "tile": "A3",
   "title": "The stacked cubes",
   "stage": "10.7",
   "newIdea": "Crash memory: it watches every spot where it hit you.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 3,
    "label": "THREE HITS, THREE SPOTS"
   },
   "params": {},
   "tale": "The cubes rose one budget at a time. Your colleague keeps records now. Never be filed in the same place.",
   "hint": "Each hit from a new spot. It remembers the old ones."
  },
  {
   "n": 8,
   "tile": "B3",
   "title": "The LED canyon",
   "stage": "10.8",
   "newIdea": "The search: lose it after being seen, and it hunts your ghost.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "hits",
    "count": 2,
    "label": "TWO HITS BY RED"
   },
   "params": {},
   "tale": "Two screens advertise to each other all night. Seen, then hidden, you are searched for. Crawl further than its curiosity.",
   "hint": "Once seen, crawl far from your ghost, or use the lure."
  },
  {
   "n": 9,
   "tile": "C3",
   "title": "The LED gateway",
   "stage": "10.9",
   "newIdea": "Two-way roads: white colleagues that never learn give moving cover.",
   "objective": {
    "kind": "stealth",
    "count": 500,
    "label": "SCORE 500 STEALTH"
   },
   "params": {},
   "tale": "The gateway thanks every car for driving safely. White colleagues never learn. Hide behind them; strike unseen for Certification.",
   "hint": "Walk beside a white car, then dash at red unseen."
  }
 ],
 "rationale": "One rung per tile, 10.1 to 10.9, never backwards. I dropped 10.10 because skipping any earlier stage gives the car a rung the player never sees it learn. Counts follow the measured open ways: 2 on teach tiles, 3 where many ways stay open (10.3, 10.4, 10.7), and back to 2 on the narrow 10.5 and 10.6. The overrides make the first side field and the first duck easier, and bring the car back sooner on 10.5. The payoff on 10.9 reopens many ways and asks for unseen strikes."
}
```
